  [Model: Claude Opus 4.5 | Temperature: 1.74 | Top P: 0.25]
  
  Genre: "dark hip hop, confessional rap, Detroit rap" Instruments: "moody piano, sparse acoustic guitar, gritty boom bap drums, layered vocal doubles, tape-saturated bass" Tags: "72 BPM; melancholic; raw; emotional; introspective"
  
  [Intro | slow build | whispered ad-libs | sparse piano | low-end rumble]
  Fuck  
  Still stuck  
  Woke up with my head in a vice, can't duck  
  Sick  
  Hands twitch  
  Nails dig in my skin, can't switch  
  Sins itch  
  Can't stitch  
  Last hit, then I’m back in the ditch  
  Shit  
  
  [Verse 1 | confessional flow | double-time syllables | tense delivery | minimal beat]
  I’m sittin’ in the dark, countin’ scars, can’t remember where the hell I started  
  Every bottle, every pill, every promise I killed, every time I said I’d quit, I just stalled it  
  My reflection’s a stranger, danger, I’m a fuckin’ hazard, tryna mask it, but I’m haunted  
  All these faces I erased, all the pain I replaced, now I’m facin’ the fact I’m unwanted  
  Mom’s voice in my head, “Marshall, you’re better than this,” but I’m deaf to the sound, I’m exhausted  
  Tryna patch up the past, but the cracks in the glass just remind me I’m constantly taunted  
  My daughter’s eyes, I see the lies, I feed her, I’m a coward, I’m a fraud, I’m dishonest  
  I’m a prisoner, sittin’ in a cell I built, every guilt trip’s a mile, I’m lost in it  
  Can’t sleep, can’t eat, can’t breathe, can’t speak, just repeat, on repeat, I’m nauseous  
  Every handshake, fake, every smile, snake, every friend I betrayed, I’m cautious  
  I’m a fuck-up, I know it, I show it, I own it, but I choke on the words when I’m on it  
  Try to run from the pain, but the pain’s in my veins, and it stains every page that I vomit  
  All the bridges I torched, all the doors that I forced, now I’m stuck in a room with the torment  
  Every night, I’m awake, tryna pray, but I break, and I ache, and I shake, and I forfeit  
  I’m a shell of a man, with a hell of a plan, but I fell, and I can’t even stand, I’m abhorrent  
  Every second I’m alive, I’m reminded I survived, but I’m dying inside, and I’m dormant  
  I’m a liar, a thief, a disease, I believe, I’m diseased, and I bleed out the torment  
  I’m a needle in a haystack, way back, laid flat, brain cracked, can’t act, just absorb it  
  I’m a punchline, fuckin’ up the timeline, rewind, but I find I’m aligned with the torment
